        function getSiteType() {
            var siteType = 'd';
            var screenWidth = screen.width;
            if (screenWidth < 768) {
                siteType = 'm';
            } else if (screenWidth < 992) {
                siteType = 't';
            }
            return siteType;
        }

        function getIdFirstProducts(count) {
            var productsGrid = $('.catalog-category-view .result-search'),
                productsId = [];
            for (var i=0; i<count; i++) {
                var product = productsGrid.find('.product-card:eq(' + i + ')'),
                id = product.data('id');
                productsId.push(id);
            }
            return productsId;
        }

        function getFormattedPrice(price) {
            var formattedPrice = 0.00;
            if(price != '') {
                formattedPrice = price.replace(/[^,0-9]/g, '')
                    .replace(',', '.');
            }
            return formattedPrice;
        }

        function getCartContent() {
            var cartContent = [],
                cart = $('.checkout-cart-index .cart-form');
            if(cart.length) {
                cart.find('.product-table--cart .product-table__row:not(.product-table__row--header)').each(function(){
                    var $this = $(this);
                    if(!$this.hasClass('product-table__row--gift')) {
                        var product = {};
                        product.id = $this.data('id');
                        product.price = parseFloat(getFormattedPrice($this.find('.product-table-price-num:not(.hidden)').text()));
                        product.quantity = parseInt($this.find('.change-qty__value').val());

                        cartContent.push(product);
                    }
                });
            }
            return cartContent;
        }

        function getPageTypeEvent(fullActionName) {
            switch(fullActionName) {
                case 'cms_index_index':
                    return {event: "viewHome"};
                case 'catalog_category_view':
                case 'catalogsearch_result_index':
                    return {event: "viewList", item: getIdFirstProducts(3)};
                case 'catalog_product_view':
                    return {event: "viewItem", item: ""};
                case 'checkout_cart_index':
                    return {event: "viewBasket", item: getCartContent()};
                case 'checkout_onepage_success':
                    return {event: "trackTransaction" };
                default:
                    return null;
            }
        }

        $(document).ready(function(){
            var criteoAccountId = 38992,

<script>
        window.vkAsyncInit = function() {
            var pixelId = document.getElementById("vk-pixel").textContent,
                pricelistId = document.getElementById("vk-pricelist-id").textContent,
                eventName = document.getElementById("vk-event-name").textContent,
                fullActionName = "cms_index_index";

            if(pixelId) {
                VK.Retargeting.Init(pixelId);
                isInitVkRetargeting = true;

                if(eventName) {
                    VK.Retargeting.Event(eventName);
                }

                if(fullActionName == 'checkout_onepage_success' && pricelistId) {
                    var eventParams = {};
                    VK.Retargeting.ProductEvent(pricelistId, 'purchase', eventParams);
                }
            }
        };
    </script>
